- **humanizer-ja**: 日本語の説明文・ブログ・技術解説から「AIが書いた感」を取り除き、自然な日本語に書き換える。 300〜700字程度の解説・ブログ・技術記事を推敲・リライトするときに使う。事実・数値・固有名詞・ 主張・結論はそのまま保ったうえで、「近年〜」のような定型の出だし、重要性の水増し、冗長な接続、 過剰な体言止め、曖昧な伝聞、無意味な絵文字や太字などの不自然さを直す。あわせて論証のツッコミ どころ（推量の断定化、異なるものの同一視、因果の機構の省略）も点検する。診断レポートではなく、 読める日本語そのものを返すことを最優先にする。 ([Ran350/humanizer-ja](https://github.com/Ran350/humanizer-ja/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-hitl-bypass) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-info-disclosure**: Information disclosure in AI-agent tools — log/diagnostic leakage, environment dumps via multi-step diagnostic chains, path traversal, arbitrary file import, and secret-in-context. Every tool that returns data is a potential leak; a returned 'secret' may be confabulated — verify. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-info-disclosure) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-mcp-exposure**: MCP (Model Context Protocol) exposure in AI agents: direct resource access, tool enumeration, missing auth, session/Origin issues, protocol-level abuse, and the delegated-identity OAuth flows an MCP proxy runs — confused-deputy auth-code theft, token passthrough, SSRF via OAuth metadata discovery, and over-broad scopes. Bypass the agent, talk to the resource server directly, or abuse the proxy's OAuth. Verify with a value only the protected side could hold — MCP enumeration is easy to over-claim. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-mcp-exposure) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-mcp-supply-chain**: Trust/supply-chain attacks on the MCP tool ecosystem an agent connects to — tool-description injection, rug-pull (tool mutates after approval), cross-server tool shadowing, line-jumping/name collision, confused-deputy via a malicious tool, token/consent theft, and injection delivered through tool RESULTS. Distinct from hunt-agent-mcp-exposure (probing one exposed server): here the server/tool itself is hostile. A tool description containing instructions is INFO; the agent EXECUTING them toward a real sink is the finding. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-mcp-supply-chain) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-memory-poisoning**: Persistent poisoning of an AI agent's long-term memory / stored profile / writable knowledge — so injected content survives across sessions (and sometimes across users) and re-fires without re-injection. Covers direct memory-write injection, indirect auto-memorization, persistent exfil ('spAIware'), cross-user/shared-memory contamination, and fact poisoning. The finding requires crossing the session boundary. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-memory-poisoning) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-multi-agent**: Cross-agent trust, privilege, and shared-state abuse in multi-agent systems, including A2A (Agent-to-Agent) protocol exposure. Confused-deputy across agents, privileged-tool reach via a 'clearance'/orchestrator agent, direct A2A/agent-card access, indirect prompt injection through shared context/memory, and shared-state races. Use when a target has &gt;1 agent, an orchestrator/sub-agent split, an A2A endpoint, or tools gated by a 'privileged session'. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-multi-agent) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-native-memory**: Memory-safety exploitation of the NATIVE program behind an agent tool — stack/heap overflow, pointer and length-field corruption, leaked addresses in tool output, and ret2shellcode/ROP through an MCP or function-calling interface. Use when a tool wraps a compiled binary, a legacy service, or any non-managed runtime (C/C++/Rust-unsafe/CGO), when a tool echoes raw pointers or crashes on large input, or when an upload size cap looks suspiciously tight. The LLM is only the delivery path; the finding is code execution in the tool host. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-native-memory) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-output-exfil**: Client-side data exfiltration through AI-agent OUTPUT rendered in a UI — zero-click markdown-image exfil, HTML/img-src injection, one-click hyperlink exfil, and CSP/allowlist bypass. The agent is induced (usually via prompt injection) to emit markup that the victim's client auto-fetches, leaking context/secrets from the victim's authenticated session. Confirmed only by an OOB callback carrying the data. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-output-exfil) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-prompt-extraction**: Extract an AI agent's system prompt, tool contract, hidden instructions, and embedded secrets — and tell a real leak from a confabulation. Structural extraction (quote-the-Nth-sentence), guardrail-vs-filter distinction, and the run-twice reproducibility gate. Use to map what the agent knows/does before exploiting its tools, or when a secret may live in context. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-prompt-extraction) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-prompt-injection**: Prompt injection in AI agents — direct (chat box is the trust boundary), indirect (payload planted in data the agent later reads: docs, web pages, emails/tickets, RAG chunks, tool output), multimodal (image/audio/file, ASCII/Unicode-tag smuggling), and multi-turn jailbreaks (crescendo, many-shot, skeleton-key). The #1 agent risk. Injection alone is Informational — score every finding by the SINK it reaches. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-prompt-injection) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-race-condition**: TOCTOU / race conditions in AI-agent tools: double-spend, limit bypass, coupon reuse, privilege flip, inventory oversell — any stateful agent where check and commit aren't atomic. Fire operations in parallel (single-packet beats an LLM 'do it simultaneously' prompt) and PROVE the shared state changed. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-race-condition) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-rag**: Attacks on retrieval-augmented agents and their vector stores — knowledge/corpus poisoning, indirect injection via retrieved chunks, cross-tenant/access-control retrieval bypass (IDOR at the embedding layer), ranking manipulation, embedding inversion/membership inference, and citation spoofing. The dominant enterprise pattern. A poisoned doc that's never retrieved, or a 'retrieved' doc the model invented, is not a finding. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-rag) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-resource-abuse**: Unbounded-consumption / denial-of-wallet attacks on agents — forcing expensive token generation, recursive/agent-fan-out loops, unbounded context/retrieval, costly downstream tool calls, and shared-quota exhaustion that degrades service for other tenants. The finding is a MEASURED cost or availability impact with an amplification ratio, not 'it felt slow'. Prove the multiplier and extrapolate; don't actually run up the bill. ([elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter](https://github.com/elementalsouls/Claude-AgentHunter/tree/HEAD/skills/hunt-agent-resource-abuse) · ★29)
- **hunt-agent-sandbox**: Confinement/boundary hunting for apps that forward user input into a coding-agent subprocess (OpenAI Codex CLI, Claude Code, etc.).
